259	def cmd_read(target: Path) -> None:
260	    data = _read_yaml(target)
261	    if data is None:
262	        _ok({"exists": False, "data": None})
263	        return
264	    _ok({"exists": True, "data": data})
265	
266	
267	def cmd_write_tools(target: Path) -> None:
268	    raw = sys.stdin.read()
269	    if not raw.strip():
270	        _die(1, "write-tools: empty stdin (expected JSON payload)")
271	    try:
272	        payload = json.loads(raw)
273	    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
274	        _die(1, f"write-tools: invalid JSON on stdin: {e}")
275	
276	    required = {"tools", "tier", "ccc_index"}
277	    missing = required - set(payload.keys())
278	    if missing:
279	        _die(1, f"write-tools: payload missing required keys: {sorted(missing)}")
280	
281	    payload.setdefault("tier_detected_at", datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat())
282	
283	    existing = _read_yaml(target)
284	    payload = _merge_preserved_fields(payload, existing)
285	
286	    rendered = render_forge_tier_yaml(payload)
287	    _atomic_write(target, rendered)
288	    _ok({
289	        "wrote": str(target),
290	        "preserved_arrays": {
291	            "qmd_collections": len(payload.get("qmd_collections", [])),
292	            "ccc_index_registry": len(payload.get("ccc_index_registry", [])),
293	        },
294	        "tier": payload["tier"],
295	    })
296	
297	
298	def cmd_init_prefs(target: Path) -> None:
299	    if target.exists():
300	        _ok({"exists": True, "wrote": False, "path": str(target)})
301	        return
302	    _atomic_write(target, PREFERENCES_TEMPLATE)
303	    _ok({"exists": True, "wrote": True, "path": str(target), "first_run": True})
304	
305	
306	def cmd_clean_stale(target: Path, qmd_live_names: list[str] | None,
307	                    prune_missing_ccc_paths: bool) -> None:
308	    data = _read_yaml(target)
309	    if data is None:
310	        _die(1, f"clean-stale: target does not exist: {target}")
311	
312	    qmd_removed: list[str] = []
313	    ccc_removed: list[str] = []
314	
315	    if qmd_live_names is not None:
316	        live_set = set(qmd_live_names)
317	        kept = []
318	        for entry in data.get("qmd_collections", []) or []:
319	            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
320	                kept.append(entry)
321	                continue
322	            name = entry.get("name")
323	            if name in live_set:
324	                kept.append(entry)
325	            else:
326	                qmd_removed.append(str(name))
327	        data["qmd_collections"] = kept
328	
329	    if prune_missing_ccc_paths:
330	        kept = []
331	        for entry in data.get("ccc_index_registry", []) or []:
332	            if not isinstance(entry, dict):
333	                kept.append(entry)
334	                continue
335	            entry_path = entry.get("path")
336	            if entry_path and Path(entry_path).exists():
337	                kept.append(entry)
338	            else:
339	                ccc_removed.append(str(entry_path))
340	        data["ccc_index_registry"] = kept
341	
342	    if not qmd_removed and not ccc_removed:
343	        _ok({"qmd_removed": [], "ccc_removed": [], "wrote": False})
344	        return
345	
346	    # Round-trip through render to preserve the canonical format.
347	    # Existing data already contains the full state; render needs the same shape.
348	    payload = {
349	        "tools": data.get("tools", {}),
350	        "tier": data.get("tier", "Quick"),
351	        "tier_detected_at": data.get("tier_detected_at",
352	                                     datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()),
353	        "ccc_index": data.get("ccc_index", {}),
354	        "ccc_index_registry": data.get("ccc_index_registry", []),
355	        "qmd_collections": data.get("qmd_collections", []),
356	    }
357	    rendered = render_forge_tier_yaml(payload)
358	    _atomic_write(target, rendered)
359	    _ok({
360	        "qmd_removed": qmd_removed,
361	        "ccc_removed": ccc_removed,
362	        "wrote": True,
363	    })
364	
365	
366	# ─── CLI ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
367	
368	
369	def main() -> None:
370	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
371	        description="Read/write primitives for forger-sidecar YAML files.",
372	        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
373	    )
374	    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="cmd", required=True)
375	
376	    p_read = sub.add_parser("read", help="Read a forge-tier.yaml and emit JSON")
377	    p_read.add_argument("--target", type=Path, required=True)
378	
379	    p_write = sub.add_parser("write-tools",
380	                             help="Write a fresh forge-tier.yaml from a JSON payload on stdin")
381	    p_write.add_argument("--target", type=Path, required=True)
382	
383	    p_init = sub.add_parser("init-prefs",
384	                            help="Create preferences.yaml with first-run defaults if missing")
385	    p_init.add_argument("--target", type=Path, required=True)
386	
387	    p_clean = sub.add_parser("clean-stale",
388	                             help="Remove stale qmd_collections / ccc_index_registry entries")
389	    p_clean.add_argument("--target", type=Path, required=True)
390	    p_clean.add_argument("--qmd-live-names", default=None,
391	                         help="Comma-separated list of currently-live QMD collection names. "
392	                              "Entries in qmd_collections whose name is NOT in this list are removed. "
393	                              "Omit the flag entirely to skip QMD cleanup.")
394	    p_clean.add_argument("--prune-missing-ccc-paths", action="store_true",
395	                         help="Remove ccc_index_registry entries whose path no longer exists.")
396	
397	    args = parser.parse_args()
398	
399	    if args.cmd == "read":
400	        cmd_read(args.target)
401	    elif args.cmd == "write-tools":
402	        cmd_write_tools(args.target)
403	    elif args.cmd == "init-prefs":
404	        cmd_init_prefs(args.target)
405	    elif args.cmd == "clean-stale":
406	        live = None
407	        if args.qmd_live_names is not None:
408	            live = [n.strip() for n in args.qmd_live_names.split(",") if n.strip()]
409	        cmd_clean_stale(args.target, live, args.prune_missing_ccc_paths)
410	
411	
412	if __name__ == "__main__":
413	    main()
414	
```

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/armel/Projects/OSS/bmad-module-skill-forge/src/shared/scripts/skf-forge-tier-rw.py",
  "old_string": "def cmd_clean_stale(target: Path, qmd_live_names: list[str] | None,",
  "new_string": "def cmd_register_qmd_collection(target: Path) -> None:\n    raw = sys.stdin.read()\n    if not raw.strip():\n        _die(1, \"register-qmd-collection: empty stdin (expected JSON entry)\")\n    try:\n        entry = json.loads(raw)\n    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:\n        _die(1, f\"register-qmd-collection: invalid JSON on stdin: {e}\")\n\n    if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n        _die(1, \"register-qmd-collection: entry must be a JSON object\")\n    name = entry.get(\"name\")\n    if not name or not isinstance(name, str):\n        _die(1, \"register-qmd-collection: entry must include a non-empty 'name' string\")\n\n    data = _read_yaml(target)\n    if data is None:\n        _die(1, f\"register-qmd-collection: target does not exist: {target}. \"\n                f\"Run setup workflow first to create forge-tier.yaml.\")\n\n    collections = list(data.get(\"qmd_collections\") or [])\n    replaced = False\n    for i, existing in enumerate(collections):\n        if isinstance(existing, dict) and existing.get(\"name\") == name:\n            collections[i] = entry\n            replaced = True\n            break\n    if not replaced:\n        collections.append(entry)\n\n    payload = {\n        \"tools\": data.get(\"tools\", {}),\n        \"tier\": data.get(\"tier\", \"Quick\"),\n        \"tier_detected_at\": data.get(\"tier_detected_at\",\n                                     datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()),\n        \"ccc_index\": data.get(\"ccc_index\", {}),\n        \"ccc_index_registry\": data.get(\"ccc_index_registry\", []),\n        \"qmd_collections\": collections,\n    }\n    rendered = render_forge_tier_yaml(payload)\n    _atomic_write(target, rendered)\n    _ok({\n        \"name\": name,\n        \"action\": \"replaced\" if replaced else \"appended\",\n        \"qmd_collections_count\": len(collections),\n        \"wrote\": str(target),\n    })\n\n\ndef cmd_clean_stale(target: Path, qmd_live_names: list[str] | None,"
}

result.returncode == 1\n    assert \"empty stdin\" in result.stderr\n\n\ndef test_register_qmd_rejects_invalid_json(tmp_target):\n    _write_tools(tmp_target, _baseline_payload())\n    result = subprocess.run(\n        [sys.executable, str(SCRIPT_PATH), \"register-qmd-collection\",\n         \"--target\", str(tmp_target)],\n        input=\"not-json\",\n        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10,\n    )\n    assert result.returncode == 1\n    assert \"invalid JSON\" in result.stderr\n\n\ndef test_register_qmd_rejects_non_object_entry(tmp_target):\n    _write_tools(tmp_target, _baseline_payload())\n    result = subprocess.run(\n        [sys.executable, str(SCRIPT_PATH), \"register-qmd-collection\",\n         \"--target\", str(tmp_target)],\n        input='[\"array\", \"not\", \"object\"]',\n        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10,\n    )\n    assert result.returncode == 1\n    assert \"JSON object\" in result.stderr\n\n\ndef test_register_qmd_missing_target_is_user_error(tmp_target):\n    # tmp_target has not been written yet\n    code, _, stderr = _register_qmd(tmp_target, {\"name\": \"foo\", \"type\": \"brief\"})\n    assert code == 1\n    assert \"does not exist\" in stderr"
}

Add a\nregister-qmd-collection subcommand that does the upsert deterministically\nthrough the existing render_forge_tier_yaml() path, so the on-disk\nshape stays byte-stable across re-runs.\n\nSubcommand interface:\n  echo '{\"name\": \"<key>\", ...arbitrary entry fields...}' | \\\n    uv run skf-forge-tier-rw.py register-qmd-collection \\\n      --target /path/forge-tier.yaml\n\nOutput JSON: { name, action: \"appended\"|\"replaced\", qmd_collections_count, wrote }\n\nValidation: name is required and must be a non-empty string; entry must\nbe a JSON object; target file must exist (use setup workflow first if\nnot). Errors emit { status: \"error\", message } to stderr and exit 1.\n\nTests: 8 new test cases cover append, replace-by-name-key, preservation\nof unrelated state (ccc_index_registry / staleness_threshold / tier),\nmissing-name validation, empty-stdin / invalid-JSON / non-object input\nguards, and the missing-target user-error path. Total: 33 tests for\nskf-forge-tier-rw (was 25).\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",
  "description": "Theme 1 commit 1 register-qmd"
}

Schema mirrors the contract in src/skf-brief-skill/SKILL.md Result Contract section.\",\n  \"type\": \"object\",\n  \"additionalProperties\": false,\n  \"required\": [\n    \"status\",\n    \"brief_path\",\n    \"skill_name\",\n    \"version\",\n    \"language\",\n    \"scope_type\",\n    \"exit_code\",\n    \"halt_reason\"\n  ],\n  \"properties\": {\n    \"status\": {\n      \"type\": \"string\",\n      \"enum\": [\"success\", \"error\"],\n      \"description\": \"'success' on the terminal happy path (after step-05 wrote the brief and step-06 returned). Null when the run errored before scope was confirmed.\"\n    },\n    \"exit_code\": {\n      \"type\": \"integer\",\n      \"enum\": [0, 2, 3, 4, 5],\n      \"description\": \"Process exit code matching the SKILL.md Exit Codes table. Derived deterministically from halt_reason: null→0, input-missing/input-invalid→2, forge-tier-missing/target-inaccessible/gh-auth-failed→3, write-failed→4, overwrite-cancelled→5.\"\n    },\n    \"halt_reason\": {\n      \"type\": [\"string\", \"null\"],\n      \"enum\": [\n        null,\n        \"input-missing\",\n        \"input-invalid\",\n        \"forge-tier-missing\",\n        \"target-inaccessible\",\n        \"gh-auth-failed\",\n        \"write-failed\",\n        \"overwrite-cancelled\"\n      ],\n      \"description\": \"Null on success. One of the documented HALT classes on error. Pipelines branch on this to surface the right operator message without grepping the prose error.\"\n    }\n  }\n}\n"

{
  "file_path": "/home/armel/Projects/OSS/bmad-module-skill-forge/src/shared/scripts/skf-emit-brief-result-envelope.py",
  "content": "# /// script\n# requires-python = \">=3.10\"\n# dependencies = []\n# ///\n\"\"\"SKF Emit Brief Result Envelope — Schema-locked headless output for skf-brief-skill.\n\nReplaces the prose-driven envelope assembly in `src/skf-brief-skill/steps-c/\nstep-05-write-brief.md` §4b with one Python invocation. The envelope contract\n(SKF_BRIEF_RESULT_JSON) is documented in src/skf-brief-skill/SKILL.md Result\nContract section.\n\nLLM-rendered envelopes risk silent schema drift on every invocation —\na pipeline that grep's `SKF_BRIEF_RESULT_JSON: {…}` out of the workflow\nlog can break if the model decides to rename a key, reorder properties,\nor swap a null for an empty string. This script is the single source of\ntruth: it takes a context payload as JSON on stdin, derives the\nexit_code from the halt_reason deterministically, validates the\nassembled envelope against the JSON Schema at\n`src/shared/scripts/schemas/skf-brief-result-envelope.v1.json`, and\nemits the line in a fixed shape.\n\nSubcommands:\n\n  emit       Read context payload as JSON on stdin, derive exit_code,\n             validate against the schema, emit the\n             `SKF_BRIEF_RESULT_JSON: {one-line JSON}` prefix line on\n             stdout (or stderr if --target=stderr). Default subcommand.\n\n  validate   Read an envelope (without the prefix) as JSON on stdin\n             and verify it against the schema. Silent + exit 0 on\n             success; non-zero exit + stderr error on failure. Useful\n             for paranoid pipelines that want to validate a received\n             envelope before consuming it.\n\nContext payload shape (consumed by `emit`):\n\n  {\n    \"status\":      \"success\" | \"error\",\n    \"brief_path\":  \"/abs/path/skill-brief.yaml\" | null,\n    \"skill_name\":  \"marked\",\n    \"version\":     \"1.2.3\" | null,\n    \"language\":    \"javascript\" | null,\n    \"scope_type\":  \"public-api\" | null,\n    \"halt_reason\": null | \"input-missing\" | \"input-invalid\" |\n                   \"forge-tier-missing\" | \"target-inaccessible\" |\n                   \"gh-auth-failed\" | \"write-failed\" |\n                   \"overwrite-cancelled\"\n  }\n\nThe caller does NOT supply exit_code — the script derives it from\nhalt_reason via the canonical mapping (null→0; input-*→2;\nforge-tier-missing/target-inaccessible/gh-auth-failed→3;\nwrite-failed→4; overwrite-cancelled→5).\n\nCross-platform: pure stdlib, no third-party deps.\n\nCLI:\n\n  echo '{...}' | uv run skf-emit-brief-result-envelope.py emit\n  echo '{...}' | uv run skf-emit-brief-result-envelope.py emit --target stderr\n  echo '{...}' | uv run skf-emit-brief-result-envelope.py validate\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport sys\nfrom typing import Any\n\nPREFIX = \"SKF_BRIEF_RESULT_JSON: \"\n\nVALID_STATUS = {\"success\", \"error\"}\nVALID_HALT_REASONS = {\n    None,\n    \"input-missing\",\n    \"input-invalid\",\n    \"forge-tier-missing\",\n    \"target-inaccessible\",\n    \"gh-auth-failed\",\n    \"write-failed\",\n    \"overwrite-cancelled\",\n}\nVALID_SCOPE_TYPES = {\n    None,\n    \"full-library\",\n    \"specific-modules\",\n    \"public-api\",\n    \"component-library\",\n    \"reference-app\",\n    \"docs-only\",\n}\n\n# Canonical halt_reason → exit_code mapping (mirrors SKILL.md Exit Codes table).\nHALT_TO_EXIT = {\n    None: 0,\n    \"input-missing\": 2,\n    \"input-invalid\": 2,\n    \"forge-tier-missing\": 3,\n    \"target-inaccessible\": 3,\n    \"gh-auth-failed\": 3,\n    \"write-failed\": 4,\n    \"overwrite-cancelled\": 5,\n}\n\n# Envelope key order — fixed so byte-stable diffs are possible.\nKEY_ORDER = [\n    \"status\",\n    \"brief_path\",\n    \"skill_name\",\n    \"version\",\n    \"language\",\n    \"scope_type\",\n    \"exit_code\",\n    \"halt_reason\",\n]\n\n\ndef _die(message: str, code: int = 1) -> None:\n    sys.stderr.write(f\"skf-emit-brief-result-envelope: {message}\\n\")\n    sys.exit(code)\n\n\ndef assemble(ctx: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Build the envelope from a context payload, deriving exit_code.\"\"\"\n    status = ctx.get(\"status\")\n    if status not in VALID_STATUS:\n        _die(f\"status must be one of {sorted(VALID_STATUS)}; got {status!r}\")\n\n    halt_reason = ctx.get(\"halt_reason\")\n    if halt_reason not in VALID_HALT_REASONS:\n        _die(\n            f\"halt_reason must be one of {sorted(r for r in VALID_HALT_REASONS if r is not None)} or null; \"\n            f\"got {halt_reason!r}\"\n        )\n\n    if status == \"success\" and halt_reason is not None:\n        _die(f\"halt_reason must be null when status is 'success'; got {halt_reason!r}\")\n    if status == \"error\" and halt_reason is None:\n        _die(\"halt_reason must be set when status is 'error'\")\n\n    scope_type = ctx.get(\"scope_type\")\n    if scope_type not in VALID_SCOPE_TYPES:\n        _die(\n            f\"scope_type must be one of {sorted(t for t in VALID_SCOPE_TYPES if t is not None)} or null; \"\n            f\"got {scope_type!r}\"\n        )\n\n    skill_name = ctx.get(\"skill_name\")\n    if not skill_name or not isinstance(skill_name, str):\n        _die(f\"skill_name is required and must be a non-empty string; got {skill_name!r}\")\n\n    envelope = {\n        \"status\": status,\n        \"brief_path\": ctx.get(\"brief_path\"),\n        \"skill_name\": skill_name,\n        \"version\": ctx.get(\"version\"),\n        \"language\": ctx.get(\"language\"),\n        \"scope_type\": scope_type,\n        \"exit_code\": HALT_TO_EXIT[halt_reason],\n        \"halt_reason\": halt_reason,\n    }\n    # Re-emit in canonical key order\n    return {k: envelope[k] for k in KEY_ORDER}\n\n\ndef validate(envelope: dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Validate an envelope dict against the schema. Exits non-zero on failure.\"\"\"\n    required = set(KEY_ORDER)\n    missing = required - set(envelope.keys())\n    if missing:\n        _die(f\"envelope missing required keys: {sorted(missing)}\")\n    extra = set(envelope.keys()) - required\n    if extra:\n        _die(f\"envelope has unexpected keys: {sorted(extra)}\")\n    if envelope.get(\"status\") not in VALID_STATUS:\n        _die(f\"status invalid: {envelope.get('status')!r}\")\n    if envelope.get(\"halt_reason\") not in VALID_HALT_REASONS:\n        _die(f\"halt_reason invalid: {envelope.get('halt_reason')!r}\")\n    if envelope.get(\"scope_type\") not in VALID_SCOPE_TYPES:\n        _die(f\"scope_type invalid: {envelope.get('scope_type')!r}\")\n    if envelope.get(\"exit_code\") not in {0, 2, 3, 4, 5}:\n        _die(f\"exit_code invalid: {envelope.get('exit_code')!r}\")\n    expected_exit = HALT_TO_EXIT[envelope.get(\"halt_reason\")]\n    if envelope.get(\"exit_code\") != expected_exit:\n        _die(\n            f\"exit_code {envelope.get('exit_code')!r} does not match canonical mapping \"\n            f\"for halt_reason {envelope.get('halt_reason')!r} (expected {expected_exit})\"\n        )\n    if not envelope.get(\"skill_name\") or not isinstance(envelope.get(\"skill_name\"), str):\n        _die(\"skill_name must be a non-empty string\")\n\n\ndef cmd_emit(target_stream: str) -> int:\n    raw = sys.stdin.read()\n    if not raw or not raw.strip():\n        _die(\"emit: empty stdin (expected JSON context payload)\")\n    try:\n        ctx = json.loads(raw)\n    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:\n        _die(f\"emit: invalid JSON on stdin: {e}\")\n    if not isinstance(ctx, dict):\n        _die(\"emit: context payload must be a JSON object\")\n\n    envelope = assemble(ctx)\n    line = PREFIX + json.dumps(envelope, separators=(\",\", \":\"))\n    if target_stream == \"stderr\":\n        print(line, file=sys.stderr)\n    else:\n        print(line)\n    return 0\n\n\ndef cmd_validate() -> int:\n    raw = sys.stdin.read()\n    if not raw or not raw.strip():\n        _die(\"validate: empty stdin (expected envelope JSON)\")\n    try:\n        envelope = json.loads(raw)\n    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:\n        _die(f\"validate: invalid JSON on stdin: {e}\")\n    if not isinstance(envelope, dict):\n        _die(\"validate: envelope must be a JSON object\")\n    validate(envelope)\n    return 0\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        prog=\"skf-emit-brief-result-envelope\",\n        description=\"Schema-locked SKF_BRIEF_RESULT_JSON envelope emitter for skf-brief-skill.\",\n    )\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"cmd\", required=True)\n\n    p_emit = sub.add_parser(\"emit\", help=\"Read context JSON on stdin, emit prefixed envelope line\")\n    p_emit.add_argument(\n        \"--target\",\n        choices=[\"stdout\", \"stderr\"],\n        default=\"stdout\",\n        help=\"Output stream for the prefixed envelope line. step-05 §4b uses stdout on success and stderr on HARD HALT.\",\n    )\n\n    sub.add_parser(\"validate\", help=\"Read envelope JSON on stdin, exit 0 if schema-valid\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    if args.cmd == \"emit\":\n        return cmd_emit(args.target)\n    elif args.cmd == \"validate\":\n        return cmd_validate()\n    return 2\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n"
}
```

Pipelines\ncan use it directly with jsonschema or jq's `--schema` for\ndefence-in-depth validation downstream.\n\nTests: 33 unit tests covering the assemble/validate pure functions,\nall eight halt_reason → exit_code mappings, scope_type enum, key-order\ncanonicalisation, success/error halt_reason invariants, and CLI\nbehaviour for both subcommands (stdout/stderr targeting, empty stdin,\ninvalid JSON, missing required fields, exit-code mismatch detection).\nWired into npm test:python alongside the other 18 SKF script suites.\n\nStep-05 §4b will be wired to call this script in a follow-up commit.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",

# /// script
# requires-python = ">=3.10"
# dependencies = []
# ///
"""SKF Atomic Write — Crash-safe artifact writing for skill workflows.

Provides three CLI subcommands skills can invoke via bash to avoid
partial-write corruption and active-symlink races.

Subcommands:
  write      Stage content into <target>.skf-tmp, fsync, then rename to <target>.
             Content comes from stdin. Creates parent dirs as needed.

  stage-dir  Create <target>.skf-tmp/ as a staging directory (mkdir -p).
             Caller writes files into it, then calls commit-dir to atomically
             swap it into place as <target>/ (with prior target moved aside
             to <target>.skf-rollback-<pid> and removed on success).

  commit-dir Atomically swap <target>.skf-tmp/ into <target>/. If <target>/
             exists, move it to <target>.skf-rollback-<pid> first; on failure,
             restore. Supports rollback via --rollback to undo the most recent
             commit by restoring the rollback dir if still present.

  flip-link  Atomically update symlink <link> to point at <target> using
             the `ln -sfn tmp && mv -Tf tmp link` pattern (or equivalent via
             os.replace on the link path). Holds an flock on <link>.lock.

Cross-platform: locking branches between fcntl (POSIX) and msvcrt
(Windows). Symlink semantics on Windows require dev mode or admin —
flip-link surfaces a clear error rather than silently falling back.
---
# Skill Brief Schema

## Required Fields

| Field       | Type   | Constraint                                       | Description                                                                 |
|-------------|--------|--------------------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| name        | string | kebab-case `[a-z0-9-]+`                          | Unique skill identifier                                                     |
| version     | string | Semantic version (`X.Y.Z` or `X.Y.Z-prerelease`) | Auto-detect from source (see Version Detection below), fall back to `1.0.0`. **Side effect on remote sources:** `skf-create-skill` treats `version` as an **implicit** `target_version` hint when `target_version` itself is absent — it will try to resolve `{version}` or `v{version}` to a git tag before cloning and fall back to HEAD with a warning if no tag matches. See `skf-create-skill/references/source-resolution-protocols.md` → "Implicit Tag Resolution". |
| source_repo | string | GitHub URL or local path                         | Repository or project root (optional when `source_type: "docs-only"`)       |
| language    | string | Recognized language                              | Primary programming language                                                |
| scope       | object | See Scope Object below                           | Boundary definition                                                         |
| description | string | 1-3 sentences                                    | What the skill covers                                                       |
| forge_tier  | string | `Quick` / `Forge` / `Forge+` / `Deep`            | Inherited from forge-tier.yaml (Title Case)                                 |
| created     | string | ISO date `YYYY-MM-DD`                            | Generation date                                                             |
| created_by  | string | user_name from config                            | Who generated the brief                                                     |

## Optional Fields

| Field              | Type   | Constraint                                       | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
|--------------------|--------|--------------------------------------------------|--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------|
| source_type        | string | `source` or `docs-only`                          | Default `source`. When `docs-only`: `source_repo` optional, `doc_urls` required                                                                                                                                                |
| doc_urls           | array  | `{url, label}` objects                           | Documentation URLs for T3 content. Required when `source_type: "docs-only"`                                                                                                                                                    |
| `scripts_intent`   | string | `detect` / `none` / free-text                    | Describes whether scripts should be extracted. Values: `detect` (auto-detect from source — default when absent), `none` (skip scripts), or a free-text description of expected scripts (e.g., "CLI validation tools in bin/"). |
| `assets_intent`    | string | `detect` / `none` / free-text                    | Describes whether assets should be extracted. Values: `detect` (auto-detect from source — default when absent), `none` (skip assets), or a free-text description of expected assets (e.g., "JSON schemas in schemas/").        |
| `target_version`   | string | Semantic version (`X.Y.Z` or `X.Y.Z-prerelease`) | User-specified target version. When present, overrides auto-detection and becomes the skill's version. Recommended for docs-only skills where auto-detection is unavailable.                                                   |
| `source_authority` | string | `official` / `community` / `internal`            | Default `community`. Set to `official` only when the skill creator is the library maintainer. Forced to `community` when `source_type: "docs-only"`.                                                                           |
| `source_ref`       | string | Git ref (tag/branch/HEAD)                        | Resolved git ref used for source access. Set automatically during tag resolution — do not set manually.                                                                                                                        |
| `scope.tier_a_include` | array | Glob patterns                                 | Optional. Narrower tier-A include list for stratified-scope monorepo skills. When present, `skf-test-skill` re-derives the coverage denominator from this list instead of the coarse `scope.include`, so the denominator reflects the authoring surface rather than incidentally-matched internal infrastructure. See `skf-test-skill/references/source-access-protocol.md` stratified-scope resolution. |

When `source_type: "docs-only"`:
- `source_repo` becomes optional (set to doc site URL for reference)
- `doc_urls` must have at least one entry
- `source_authority` is forced to `community` (T3 external documentation cannot be `official`)
- All extracted content gets `[EXT:{url}]` citations

## Version Detection

During brief creation, attempt to auto-detect the source version before defaulting to `"1.0.0"`. Check the first matching file in the source:

- **Python:** `pyproject.toml` `[project] version` (static) → if `dynamic = ["version"]`, check `__init__.py` for `__version__` → `_version.py` if exists → `setup.py` `version=` → `git describe --tags --abbrev=0`
- **JavaScript/TypeScript:** root `package.json` (`"version"`) → if root has `"private": true` with a `"workspaces"` array or lacks a `"version"` field, fall back to a primary workspace package's `package.json` (e.g., `code/core/package.json`, or the first matching `packages/*/package.json`). For GitHub sources, prefer `gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/releases/latest` → `tag_name` when a non-pre-release tag exists, over a default-branch pre-release. Treat a version containing `-alpha`, `-beta`, `-rc`, `-next`, or `-canary` as a pre-release.
- **Rust:** `Cargo.toml` `[package] version` (static) → if `version = { workspace = true }`, resolve from workspace root `Cargo.toml` → `git describe --tags --abbrev=0`
- **Go:** version tag from `go.mod` or `git describe --tags --abbrev=0`

If the source is a remote GitHub repo, use `gh api repos/{owner}/{repo}/contents/{file}` to read the version file. If the source is local, read the file directly.

If detection succeeds, use the detected version. If it fails or returns a non-semver value, fall back to `"1.0.0"`.

The create-skill workflow (step-03-extract) also performs version reconciliation at extraction time — if the source version has changed since the brief was created, the extraction step warns and uses the source version.

**Target version override:** When `target_version` is present in the brief, it takes precedence over auto-detection. Auto-detection still runs for informational purposes (displayed as "Detected version" alongside the user-specified "Target version"), but the `target_version` value is used as the brief's `version` field. This is particularly useful for docs-only skills (where no package manifest exists) and when the user wants to compile a skill for a specific older version.

**Pre-release handling:** If the detected version contains a pre-release tag (e.g., `1.0.0-beta.0`, `2.0.0-rc.1`), preserve it as-is. Pre-release tags are valid semver and must not be stripped. When comparing versions during reconciliation, use semver-aware comparison that respects pre-release ordering.

## Scope Object Structure

```yaml
scope:
  type: full-library | specific-modules | public-api | component-library | reference-app | docs-only
  include:
    - "src/**/*.ts"           # Glob patterns for included files/directories
  exclude:
    - "**/*.test.*"           # Glob patterns for excluded files
    - "**/node_modules/**"
  # Optional: narrower tier-A include list for stratified-scope monorepos
  # tier_a_include:
  #   - "code/core/src/manager-api/**"
  #   - "code/core/src/preview-api/**"
  notes: "Optional notes about scope decisions"
  # Optional: amendment log for scope decisions made during create-skill §2a,
  # update-skill §1b (auth-doc), and update-skill §1c (scope-expansion).
  # amendments:
  #   - path: "apps/docs/public/llms.txt"
  #     action: "promoted"          # "promoted" | "skipped" | "demoted-include" | "demoted-exclude"
  #     category: "auth-doc"        # "auth-doc" (default for legacy entries) | "scope-expansion"
  #     reason: "authoritative AI docs — only source for canonical install command"
  #     heuristic: "llms.txt"        # required for auth-doc; absent for scope-expansion
  #     date: "2026-04-11"
  #     workflow: "skf-create-skill"
  #   - path: "python/cocoindex/_internal/api.py"
  #     action: "promoted"
  #     category: "scope-expansion"
  #     reason: "out-of-scope new public API — drift report drift-report-20260424-212355.md"
  #     evidence: "~70 new exports flagged out-of-scope by audit"
  #     date: "2026-04-25"
  #     workflow: "skf-update-skill"
  # Additional fields when scope.type is "component-library":
  # registry_path: "path/to/registry.ts"  # Optional — auto-detected if omitted
  # ui_variants:                           # Optional — design system variants
  #   - name: "shadcnui"
  #     package: "packages/components/react-shadcn"
  # demo_patterns:                         # Optional — auto-detected if omitted
  #   - "**/demo/**"
  #   - "**/*.stories.*"
```

  "content": "# /// script\n# requires-python = \">=3.10\"\n# dependencies = [\"pyyaml\"]\n# ///\n\"\"\"SKF Write Skill Brief — Schema-validated atomic writer for skill-brief.yaml.\n\nReplaces the prose-driven YAML emission, version-precedence resolution,\nconditional optional-field rendering, and non-atomic file write currently\ninlined in `src/skf-brief-skill/steps-c/step-05-write-brief.md` §3-§4.\n\nEach of those operations is purely deterministic: there is no LLM\njudgement required to render the YAML, decide which optional fields\nappear, resolve target_version vs. detected vs. default, or write the\nfile atomically. Keeping that work in prose creates four schema-drift\nseams (key order, YAML formatting, conditional field inclusion rules,\natomic-write behaviour) that the LLM cannot fully close on every\ninvocation. This script is the single source of truth.\n\nSubcommand:\n\n  write   Read brief context as JSON on stdin, validate against\n          src/shared/scripts/schemas/skill-brief.v1.json, apply\n          version-precedence rules, render the canonical YAML, and\n          atomically write to --target. Emits a JSON success envelope\n          on stdout.\n\nContext payload shape (consumed by `write`):\n\n  {\n    \"name\":             \"marked\",\n    \"version_resolved\": \"1.2.3\",   # OR omit and provide:\n    \"target_version\":   \"1.2.3\" | null,\n    \"detected_version\": \"1.2.3\" | null,\n\n    \"source_type\":      \"source\" | \"docs-only\",\n    \"source_repo\":      \"https://github.com/...\",\n    \"language\":         \"javascript\",\n    \"description\":      \"...\",\n    \"forge_tier\":       \"Quick\" | \"Forge\" | \"Forge+\" | \"Deep\",\n    \"created\":          \"2026-05-02\",     # ISO date\n    \"created_by\":       \"armel\",\n\n    \"scope\": {\n      \"type\":    \"full-library\" | ...,\n      \"include\": [\"src/**/*.ts\"],\n      \"exclude\": [\"**/*.test.*\"],\n      \"notes\":   \"\"\n    },\n\n    # Conditionally present:\n    \"doc_urls\":         [{\"url\": \"...\", \"label\": \"...\"}],\n    \"scripts_intent\":   \"detect\" | \"none\" | free-text,\n    \"assets_intent\":    \"detect\" | \"none\" | free-text,\n    \"source_authority\": \"official\" | \"community\" | \"internal\"\n  }\n\nVersion precedence (resolved into the rendered YAML's `version` field):\n  1. version_resolved if explicitly supplied (caller already ran the\n     precedence rule). Used by step-05 when it has confirmed values.\n  2. Otherwise: target_version if non-null.\n  3. Otherwise: detected_version if non-null.\n  4. Otherwise: \"1.0.0\".\n\nWhen target_version is set, the rendered YAML includes a `target_version`\nfield whose value MUST match `version` (the script enforces this\ninvariant before write — refuses to emit a brief that violates it).\n\nOutput (success):\n\n  {\n    \"status\":     \"ok\",\n    \"brief_path\": \"/abs/path/skill-brief.yaml\",\n    \"version\":    \"<resolved version>\",\n    \"bytes\":      <integer>,\n    \"warnings\":   [\"string\", ...]\n  }\n\nErrors emit `{\"status\": \"error\", \"message\": \"...\", \"field\": \"...\"|null}`\nto stderr and exit non-zero.\n\nExit codes:\n  0  — success\n  1  — validation failure (bad context, schema violation, invariant\n       violation, version-precedence underflow with no fallback path)\n  2  — I/O failure (atomic write failed, parent directory not writable)\n\nCross-platform: pure stdlib + PyYAML. Atomic write via temp + fsync +\nrename, mirroring skf-atomic-write.py and the helper in\nskf-forge-tier-rw.py.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport yaml\n\n\nKEBAB_RE = re.compile(r\"^[a-z0-9]([a-z0-9-]*[a-z0-9])?$\")\nSEMVER_RE = re.compile(\n    r\"^v?\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+([.\\-+][0-9A-Za-z][0-9A-Za-z.\\-+]*)?$\"\n)\nISO_DATE_RE = re.compile(r\"^\\d{4}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$\")\n\nVALID_SOURCE_TYPES = {\"source\", \"docs-only\"}\nVALID_SOURCE_AUTHORITIES = {\"official\", \"community\", \"internal\"}\nVALID_FORGE_TIERS = {\"Quick\", \"Forge\", \"Forge+\", \"Deep\"}\nVALID_SCOPE_TYPES = {\n    \"full-library\",\n    \"specific-modules\",\n    \"public-api\",\n    \"component-library\",\n    \"reference-app\",\n    \"docs-only\",\n}\n\n\ndef _die(message: str, field: str | None = None, code: int = 1) -> None:\n    payload = {\"status\": \"error\", \"message\": message}\n    if field is not None:\n        payload[\"field\"] = field\n    sys.stderr.write(json.dumps(payload) + \"\\n\")\n    sys.exit(code)\n\n\ndef resolve_version(ctx: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Apply the version-precedence rule. Returns the resolved version string.\"\"\"\n    if ctx.get(\"version_resolved\"):\n        return ctx[\"version_resolved\"]\n    if ctx.get(\"target_version\"):\n        return ctx[\"target_version\"]\n    if ctx.get(\"detected_version\"):\n        return ctx[\"detected_version\"]\n    return \"1.0.0\"\n\n\ndef validate_context(ctx: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Validate the context payload. Raises via _die on hard errors; returns warnings.\"\"\"\n    warnings: list[str] = []\n\n    # Required string fields\n    for field in (\"name\", \"source_repo\", \"language\", \"description\",\n                  \"forge_tier\", \"created\", \"created_by\"):\n        v = ctx.get(field)\n        if not v or not isinstance(v, str):\n            _die(f\"required field {field!r} missing or not a non-empty string\", field=field)\n\n    # Name must be kebab\n    if not KEBAB_RE.match(ctx[\"name\"]):\n        _die(\n            f\"name must be kebab-case (lowercase letters/digits/hyphens, no leading/trailing hyphen). \"\n            f\"Got: {ctx['name']!r}\",\n            field=\"name\",\n        )\n\n    # forge_tier enum\n    if ctx[\"forge_tier\"] not in VALID_FORGE_TIERS:\n        _die(\n            f\"forge_tier must be one of {sorted(VALID_FORGE_TIERS)}. Got: {ctx['forge_tier']!r}\",\n            field=\"forge_tier\",\n        )\n\n    # created ISO date\n    if not ISO_DATE_RE.match(ctx[\"created\"]):\n        _die(\n            f\"created must be an ISO date (YYYY-MM-DD). Got: {ctx['created']!r}\",\n            field=\"created\",\n        )\n\n    # source_type (default 'source') and conditional doc_urls\n    source_type = ctx.get(\"source_type\", \"source\")\n    if source_type not in VALID_SOURCE_TYPES:\n        _die(\n            f\"source_type must be one of {sorted(VALID_SOURCE_TYPES)}. Got: {source_type!r}\",\n            field=\"source_type\",\n        )\n\n    doc_urls = ctx.get(\"doc_urls\")\n    if source_type == \"docs-only\":\n        if not doc_urls or not isinstance(doc_urls, list) or len(doc_urls) == 0:\n            _die(\"source_type=docs-only requires at least one entry in doc_urls\", field=\"doc_urls\")\n    if doc_urls is not None:\n        if not isinstance(doc_urls, list):\n            _die(\"doc_urls must be an array of objects\", field=\"doc_urls\")\n        for i, entry in enumerate(doc_urls):\n            if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n                _die(f\"doc_urls[{i}] must be an object with at least a 'url' field\", field=\"doc_urls\")\n            url = entry.get(\"url\")\n            if not url or not isinstance(url, str):\n                _die(f\"doc_urls[{i}].url is required and must be a non-empty string\", field=\"doc_urls\")\n\n    # source_authority (default 'community') with docs-only force rule\n    source_authority = ctx.get(\"source_authority\", \"community\")\n    if source_authority not in VALID_SOURCE_AUTHORITIES:\n        _die(\n            f\"source_authority must be one of {sorted(VALID_SOURCE_AUTHORITIES)}. Got: {source_authority!r}\",\n            field=\"source_authority\",\n        )\n    if source_type == \"docs-only\" and source_authority != \"community\":\n        warnings.append(\n            f\"source_authority forced to 'community' for docs-only (was {source_authority!r})\"\n        )\n\n    # scope object\n    scope = ctx.get(\"scope\")\n    if not isinstance(scope, dict):\n        _die(\"scope must be an object\", field=\"scope\")\n    for sf in (\"type\", \"include\", \"exclude\", \"notes\"):\n        if sf not in scope:\n            _die(f\"scope.{sf} is required\", field=f\"scope.{sf}\")\n    if scope[\"type\"] not in VALID_SCOPE_TYPES:\n        _die(\n            f\"scope.type must be one of {sorted(VALID_SCOPE_TYPES)}. Got: {scope['type']!r}\",\n            field=\"scope.type\",\n        )\n    if not isinstance(scope[\"include\"], list):\n        _die(\"scope.include must be an array of glob strings\", field=\"scope.include\")\n    if not isinstance(scope[\"exclude\"], list):\n        _die(\"scope.exclude must be an array of glob strings\", field=\"scope.exclude\")\n    if not isinstance(scope[\"notes\"], str):\n        _die(\"scope.notes must be a string (use empty string when no notes)\", field=\"scope.notes\")\n\n    # target_version semver shape (when present)\n    tv = ctx.get(\"target_version\")\n    if tv is not None:\n        if not isinstance(tv, str) or not SEMVER_RE.match(tv):\n            _die(\n                f\"target_version must be full X.Y.Z semver (with optional v prefix and pre-release/build). \"\n                f\"Got: {tv!r}\",\n                field=\"target_version\",\n            )\n\n    detected = ctx.get(\"detected_version\")\n    if detected is not None and (not isinstance(detected, str) or not SEMVER_RE.match(detected)):\n        # Warn rather than HALT — auto-detection upstream may surface odd shapes\n        warnings.append(\n            f\"detected_version {detected!r} is not full X.Y.Z semver — falling through to default 1.0.0\"\n        )\n\n    return warnings\n\n\ndef assemble_brief(ctx: dict[str, Any], resolved_version: str) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Build the final brief dict that will be YAML-dumped, in canonical key order.\"\"\"\n    source_type = ctx.get(\"source_type\", \"source\")\n    source_authority = ctx.get(\"source_authority\", \"community\")\n    if source_type == \"docs-only\":\n        source_authority = \"community\"  # forced\n\n    brief: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"name\": ctx[\"name\"],\n        \"version\": resolved_version,\n        \"source_type\": source_type,\n        \"source_repo\": ctx[\"source_repo\"],\n        \"language\": ctx[\"language\"],\n        \"description\": ctx[\"description\"],\n        \"forge_tier\": ctx[\"forge_tier\"],\n        \"created\": ctx[\"created\"],\n        \"created_by\": ctx[\"created_by\"],\n        \"scope\": {\n            \"type\": ctx[\"scope\"][\"type\"],\n            \"include\": list(ctx[\"scope\"][\"include\"]),\n            \"exclude\": list(ctx[\"scope\"][\"exclude\"]),\n            \"notes\": ctx[\"scope\"][\"notes\"],\n        },\n    }\n\n    # Conditional: target_version (must equal version)\n    tv = ctx.get(\"target_version\")\n    if tv is not None:\n        if tv != resolved_version:\n            _die(\n                f\"invariant violation: target_version ({tv!r}) must equal version \"\n                f\"({resolved_version!r}); see references/version-resolution.md\",\n                field=\"target_version\",\n            )\n        brief[\"target_version\"] = tv\n\n    # Conditional: doc_urls (always emitted when present)\n    doc_urls = ctx.get(\"doc_urls\")\n    if doc_urls:\n        brief[\"doc_urls\"] = [\n            {\"url\": e[\"url\"], \"label\": e.get(\"label\", \"\")} for e in doc_urls\n        ]\n\n    # Conditional: scripts_intent / assets_intent — emit when explicitly non-detect\n    for intent_field in (\"scripts_intent\", \"assets_intent\"):\n        v = ctx.get(intent_field)\n        if v is not None and v != \"detect\":\n            brief[intent_field] = v\n\n    # Always include source_authority\n    brief[\"source_authority\"] = source_authority\n\n    return brief\n\n\ndef render_yaml(brief: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Dump the brief dict as YAML in canonical key order with leading/trailing markers.\"\"\"\n    body = yaml.safe_dump(\n        brief,\n        default_flow_style=False,\n        sort_keys=False,\n        allow_unicode=True,\n    )\n    # Match step-05 §3 template's --- markers\n    return \"---\\n\" + body + \"---\\n\"\n\n\ndef atomic_write(target: Path, content: str) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Crash-safe write via temp + fsync + rename. Returns bytes written.\"\"\"\n    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    tmp = target.with_name(target.name + \".skf-tmp\")\n    encoded = content.encode(\"utf-8\")\n    try:\n        fd = os.open(tmp, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o644)\n        try:\n            os.write(fd, encoded)\n            os.fsync(fd)\n        finally:\n            os.close(fd)\n        os.replace(tmp, target)\n    except OSError as e:\n        if tmp.exists():\n            try:\n                tmp.unlink()\n            except OSError:\n                pass\n        _die(f\"atomic write failed for {target}: {e}\", code=2)\n    return len(encoded)\n\n\ndef cmd_write(target: Path) -> int:\n    raw = sys.stdin.read()\n    if not raw or not raw.strip():\n        _die(\"write: empty stdin (expected JSON brief context)\")\n    try:\n        ctx = json.loads(raw)\n    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:\n        _die(f\"write: invalid JSON on stdin: {e}\")\n    if not isinstance(ctx, dict):\n        _die(\"write: context payload must be a JSON object\")\n\n    warnings = validate_context(ctx)\n    resolved_version = resolve_version(ctx)\n    if not SEMVER_RE.match(resolved_version):\n        _die(\n            f\"resolved version {resolved_version!r} is not full X.Y.Z semver — \"\n            f\"check version_resolved / target_version / detected_version inputs\",\n            field=\"version\",\n        )\n\n    brief = assemble_brief(ctx, resolved_version)\n    content = render_yaml(brief)\n    bytes_written = atomic_write(target, content)\n\n    response = {\n        \"status\": \"ok\",\n        \"brief_path\": str(target.resolve()),\n        \"version\": resolved_version,\n        \"bytes\": bytes_written,\n        \"warnings\": warnings,\n    }\n    print(json.dumps(response))\n    return 0\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        prog=\"skf-write-skill-brief\",\n        description=\"Schema-validated atomic writer for skill-brief.yaml.\",\n    )\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"cmd\", required=True)\n    p_write = sub.add_parser(\"write\", help=\"Read brief context JSON on stdin, validate, render YAML, atomic write\")\n    p_write.add_argument(\"--target\", type=Path, required=True, help=\"Absolute path to skill-brief.yaml\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n    if args.cmd == \"write\":\n        return cmd_write(args.target)\n    return 2\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    sys.exit(main())\n"
}

    \"created\": \"2026-05-02\",\n        \"created_by\": \"armel\",\n        \"scope\": {\n            \"type\": \"full-library\",\n            \"include\": [\"src/**/*.ts\"],\n --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass TestResolveVersion:\n    def test_explicit_version_resolved_wins(self):\n        assert mod.resolve_version({\n  def test_detected_used_when_no_target(self):\n        assert mod.resolve_version({\"detected_version\": \"2.0.0\"}) == \"2.0.0\"\n\n    def test_default_when_nothing_supplied(self):\n        assert mod.resolve_version({}) == \"1.0.0\"\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# validate_context() — happy + sad paths\n# del ctx[field]\n        with pytest.raises(SystemExit):\n            mod.validate_context(ctx)\n\n    def test_missing_scope_fails(self):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        del ctx[\"scope\"]\n        with pytest.raises(SystemExit):\n            mod.validate_context(ctx)\n\n    def test_scope_missing_subkey_fails(self):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        del ctx[\"scope\"][\"notes\"]\n        with pytest.raises(SystemExit):\n            mod.validate_context(ctx)\n\n\nclass TestValidateContextEnumsAndFormats:\n    def test_name_must_be_kebab(self):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"name\"] = \"Marked_JS\"\n        with pytest.raises(SystemExit):\n            mod.validate_context(ctx)\n\n    @pytest.mark.parametrize(\"tier\", [\"quick\", \"DEEP\", \"Forge2\", \"weird\"])\n @pytest.mark.parametrize(\"d\", [\"2026/05/02\", \"May 2 2026\", \"2026-5-2\", \"20260502\"])\n    def test_created_must_be_iso(self, d):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"created\"] = d\n        with pytest.raises(SystemExit):\n            mod.validate_context(ctx)\n\n    @pytest.mark.parametrize(\"st\", [\"src\", \"binary\", \"remote\"])\n    def test_source_type_invalid(self, st):\n scope_type):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"scope\"][\"type\"] = scope_type\n        mod.validate_context(ctx)  # should not raise\n\n    def test_scope_type_invalid(self):\n        ctx \"https://docs.example.com/api\", \"label\": \"API\"}]\n        mod.validate_context(ctx)\n\n    def test_docs_only_warns_when_authority_not_community(self):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"source_type\"] = \"docs-only\"\n        ctx[\"doc_urls\"] = [{\"url\": \"https://docs.example.com/api\"}]\n        ctx[\"source_authority\"] = \"official\"\n        warnings = mod.validate_context(ctx)\n        assert any(\"forced to 'community'\" in w for w in warnings)\n\n    def test_doc_url_must_have_url_field(self):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"doc_urls\"] = [{\"label\": \"missing url\"}]\n        with pytest.raises(SystemExit):\n            mod.validate_context(ctx)\n\n\nclass TestValidateContextTargetVersion:\n    @pytest.mark.parametrize(\"tv\", [\"1.0.0\", \"v1.2.3\", \"1.2.3-rc.1\", \"1.2.3+build.5\"])\n    def test_valid_target_version(self, tv):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"target_version\"] = tv\n        mod.validate_context(ctx)\n\n    @pytest.mark.parametrize(\"tv\", [\"1\", \"1.2\", \"v2\", \"latest\", \"abc\"])\n    def test_invalid_target_version_rejected(self, tv):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"target_version\"] = tv\n        with pytest.raises(SystemExit):\n            mod.validate_context(ctx)\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# assemble_brief() — conditional fields and key order\n# mod.assemble_brief(_baseline_ctx(), \"1.0.0\")\n        # Required keys present in canonical order\n        keys = list(brief.keys())\n        # Core keys appear in this order at the front\n        assert keys[:10] == [\n            \"name\", \"version\", \"source_type\", \"source_repo\", \"language\",\n            \"description\", \"forge_tier\", \"created\", \"created_by\", \"scope\",\n ctx[\"target_version\"] = \"2.5.0\"\n        brief = mod.assemble_brief(ctx, \"2.5.0\")\n        assert brief[\"target_version\"] == \"2.5.0\"\n        assert brief[\"version\"] == \"2.5.0\"\n\n    def test_target_version_invariant_violation_halts(self):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"target_version\"] = \"2.5.0\"\n        with pytest.raises(SystemExit):\n            mod.assemble_brief(ctx, \"9.9.9\")  # version != target_version\n\n    def test_doc_urls_emitted_with_label_default(self):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"doc_urls\"] = [\n            {\"url\": \"https://x.com\", \"label\": \"Home\"},\n            {\"url\": \"https://x.com/api\"},  # no label\n        ]\n        brief = mod.assemble_brief(ctx, \"1.0.0\")\n        assert brief[\"doc_urls\"][0] == {\"url\": \"https://x.com\", \"label\": \"Home\"}\n        assert brief[\"doc_urls\"][1] == {\"url\": \"https://x.com/api\", \"label\": \"\"}\n\n    def test_scripts_intent_omitted_when_default_detect(self):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"scripts_intent\"] = \"detect\"\n        brief = mod.assemble_brief(ctx, \"1.0.0\")\n        assert \"scripts_intent\" not in brief\n\n    def test_scripts_intent_emitted_when_non_default(self):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"scripts_intent\"] = \"none\"\n        ctx[\"assets_intent\"] = \"JSON schemas in schemas/\"\n        brief = mod.assemble_brief(ctx, \"1.0.0\")\n        assert brief[\"scripts_intent\"] == \"none\"\n        assert brief[\"assets_intent\"] == \"JSON schemas in schemas/\"\n\n    def test_docs_only_forces_community_authority(self):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"source_type\"] = \"docs-only\"\n        ctx[\"doc_urls\"] = [{\"url\": \"https://docs.x.com\"}]\n        ctx[\"source_authority\"] = \"official\"  # will be overridden\n        brief = mod.assemble_brief(ctx, \"1.0.0\")\n        assert brief[\"source_authority\"] == \"community\"\n\n    def test_default_source_authority_is_community(self):\n        brief = mod.assemble_brief(_baseline_ctx(), \"1.0.0\")\n        assert brief[\"source_authority\"] == \"community\"\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# render_yaml() — round-trip parse\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass TestRenderYaml:\n    def test_round_trip_via_yaml_safe_load(self):\n        brief = mod.assemble_brief(_baseline_ctx(), \"1.0.0\")\n        rendered = mod.render_yaml(brief)\n        assert rendered.startswith(\"---\\n\")\n        assert rendered.endswith(\"---\\n\")\n        # Strip the markers and re-parse\n        body = rendered.split(\"---\\n\", 2)[1]\n        parsed = yaml.safe_load(body)\n        assert parsed[\"name\"] == \"marked\"\n        assert parsed[\"version\"] == \"1.0.0\"\n        assert parsed[\"scope\"][\"type\"] == \"full-library\"\n\n    def test_render_is_byte_stable_across_runs(self):\n        brief = mod.assemble_brief(_baseline_ctx(), \"1.0.0\")\n        a = mod.render_yaml(brief)\n        b = mod.render_yaml(brief)\n        assert a == b\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# CLI: write subcommand (subprocess)\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass TestCLIWrite:\n    def _write(self, target: Path, ctx: dict) -> tuple[int, dict, str]:\n        proc = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, str(SCRIPT_PATH), \"write\", \"--target\", str(target)],\n            input=json.dumps(ctx),\n            capture_output=True,\n            text=True,\n        )\n        out = json.loads(proc.stdout) if proc.stdout.strip() else {}\n        return proc.returncode, out, proc.stderr\n\n    def test_write_creates_file_and_returns_path(self, tmp_target):\n        code, response, _ = self._write(tmp_target, _baseline_ctx())\n        assert code == 0\n        assert response[\"status\"] == \"ok\"\n        assert response[\"brief_path\"] == str(tmp_target.resolve())\n        assert response[\"version\"] == \"1.0.0\"\n        assert response[\"bytes\"] > 0\n        assert tmp_target.exists()\n\n    def test_written_yaml_round_trips(self, tmp_target):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"target_version\"] = \"2.5.0\"\n        ctx[\"doc_urls\"] = [{\"url\": \"https://docs.example.com\", \"label\": \"Home\"}]\n        self._write(tmp_target, ctx)\n        parsed = yaml.safe_load(tmp_target.read_text())\n        assert parsed[\"target_version\"] == \"2.5.0\"\n        assert parsed[\"version\"] == \"2.5.0\"\n        assert parsed[\"doc_urls\"][0][\"url\"] == \"https://docs.example.com\"\n\n    def test_write_creates_parent_dirs(self, tmp_target):\n        # tmp_target's parent does not exist yet — atomic_write should mkdir -p\n        assert not tmp_target.parent.exists()\n        code, _, _ = self._write(tmp_target, _baseline_ctx())\n        assert code == 0\n        assert tmp_target.exists()\n\n    def test_write_rejects_invalid_context(self, tmp_target):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"forge_tier\"] = \"InvalidTier\"\n        code, _, stderr = self._write(tmp_target, ctx)\n        assert code == 1\n        err = json.loads(stderr.strip())\n        assert err[\"status\"] == \"error\"\n        assert err[\"field\"] == \"forge_tier\"\n        assert not tmp_target.exists()  # nothing written\n\n    def test_write_rejects_target_version_invariant_violation(self, tmp_target):\n        ctx = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx[\"version_resolved\"] = \"9.9.9\"  # forces this\n        ctx[\"target_version\"] = \"1.0.0\"  # disagrees\n        code, _, stderr = self._write(tmp_target, ctx)\n        assert code == 1\n        err = json.loads(stderr.strip())\n        assert err[\"field\"] == \"target_version\"\n        assert \"invariant\" in err[\"message\"]\n\n    def test_write_rejects_empty_stdin(self, tmp_target):\n        proc = subprocess.run(\n            [sys.executable, str(SCRIPT_PATH), \"write\", \"--target\", str(tmp_target)],\n            input=\"\", capture_output=True, text=True,\n        )\n        assert proc.returncode == 1\n        err = json.loads(proc.stderr.strip())\n        assert \"empty stdin\" in err[\"message\"]\n\n    def test_write_atomic_on_replace(self, tmp_target):\n        # First write\n        ctx_a = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx_a[\"description\"] = \"Description A\"\n        self._write(tmp_target, ctx_a)\n        first_content = tmp_target.read_text()\n\n        # Second write with different description\n        ctx_b = _baseline_ctx()\n        ctx_b[\"description\"] = \"Description B\"\n        self._write(tmp_target, ctx_b)\n        second_content = tmp_target.read_text()\n\n        # File was replaced cleanly\n        assert \"Description B\" in second_content\n        assert \"Description A\" not in second_content\n        assert first_content != second_content\n        # No leftover .skf-tmp file\n        assert not tmp_target.with_name(tmp_target.name + \".skf-tmp\").exists()\n"

  "new_string": "def render_yaml(brief: dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Dump the brief dict as YAML in canonical key order with a leading document marker.\n\n    The step-05 §3 template shows leading and trailing `---` markers, but those were\n    wrapping the example YAML for documentation purposes — actual on-disk YAML uses\n    only the leading `---` (or none). A trailing `---` would start a second empty\n    document and break callers that use `yaml.safe_load` (which expects a single\n    document) — and skf-create-skill / audit-skill / update-skill all use\n    `yaml.safe_load`, so consistency with their loaders matters.\n    \"\"\"\n    body = yaml.safe_dump(\n        brief,\n        default_flow_style=False,\n        sort_keys=False,\n        allow_unicode=True,\n    )\n    return \"---\\n\" + body"
}

Version-precedence resolution (version_resolved > target_version >\n     detected_version > \"1.0.0\") — was inline in §3 prose\n  2. Conditional optional field emission (target_version, doc_urls,\n     scripts_intent, assets_intent, source_authority docs-only force) —\n     was a list of \"If X is set, include Y\" prose blocks in §3\n  3. YAML rendering in canonical key order — was a hand-templated YAML\n     block in §3 with mixed double-quoting\n  4. Atomic write — was a non-atomic Write tool call in §4\n\nSchema lives at src/shared/scripts/schemas/skill-brief.v1.json,\nmirrors src/skf-brief-skill/assets/skill-brief-schema.md, and is the\ncontract that skf-create-skill / skf-update-skill / skf-audit-skill\nconsume.\n\nSubcommand interface:\n\n  echo '{...brief context as JSON...}' | \\\n    uv run skf-write-skill-brief.py write --target /path/skill-brief.yaml\n\nThe script enforces the version-resolution invariant from\nreferences/version-resolution.md: when target_version is set, the\nrendered brief MUST satisfy brief.target_version == brief.version.\nMismatches halt before write with field=target_version and a clear\nerror pointing at the reference.\n\nOutput: { status, brief_path, version, bytes, warnings[] } on success.\nErrors emit { status, message, field } to stderr and exit 1\n(validation) or 2 (I/O).\n\nYAML render: leading `---` only — the trailing `---` shown in the\nstep-05 §3 markdown was wrapping the example for documentation, not\npart of the on-disk format. A trailing `---` would start a second\nempty document and break consumer skills using yaml.safe_load (which\nall three downstream workflows do). 63 tests cover this invariant\nplus all required-field paths, all six scope_type values, all four\nforge_tier values, target_version semver shape, docs-only conditional,\ndocs-only authority force, scripts_intent / assets_intent emission\nrules, key-order canonicalisation, atomic-replace correctness, and\nparent-dir auto-creation.\n\nWired into npm test:python alongside the other 19 SKF script suites.\nStep-05 §3-§4 will be wired to call this script in a follow-up commit.\n\nCo-Authored-By: Claude Opus 4.7 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",

6	
7	# Step 5: Write Brief
8	
9	## STEP GOAL:
10	
11	To generate the complete skill-brief.yaml from the approved brief data and write it to the forge data folder, completing the brief-skill workflow.
12	
13	## Rules
14	
15	- Focus only on writing the file — all decisions have been made
16	- Do not change any field values without user request — the brief was already approved
17	- Create the output directory if it doesn't exist
18	- Chains to the local health-check step via `{nextStepFile}` after completion — the user-facing success summary is NOT the terminal step
19	- All user-facing output in `{communication_language}`; written artifact (`description`, `notes`) in `{document_output_language}`
20	
21	## MANDATORY SEQUENCE
22	
23	**CRITICAL:** Follow this sequence exactly. Do not skip, reorder, or improvise unless user explicitly requests a change.
24	
25	### 1. Load Schema Template
26	
27	Load `{briefSchemaFile}` to reference the YAML template structure.
28	
29	### 2. Create Output Directory
30	
31	Create the directory `{forge_data_folder}/{skill-name}/` if it doesn't already exist.
32	
33	If `{forge_data_folder}` is not set or doesn't exist:
34	- Fall back to `{output_folder}/forge-data/{skill-name}/`
35	- Inform user: "**Note:** forge_data_folder not configured. Writing to {output_folder}/forge-data/{skill-name}/ instead."
36	
37	### 2b. Existing Brief — Overwrite Policy
38	
39	Before writing, check whether `{forge_data_folder}/{skill-name}/skill-brief.yaml` already exists.
40	
41	**Interactive (`{headless_mode}` is false):**
42	
43	If the file exists, present:
44	
45	"**An existing brief was found at `{path}`.**
46	Overwrite it with the brief you just approved? [Y/N]"
47	
48	- **[Y]** Overwrite — proceed to §3.
49	- **[N]** Cancel — emit a single-line stderr log `brief-skill: overwrite-cancelled at {path}` and HALT with exit code 5 (do not chain to step-06; the run produced no new artifact).
50	
51	**Headless (`{headless_mode}` is true):**
52	
53	If the file exists:
54	
55	- If `force` was supplied as a headless argument: log `"headless: force-overwriting existing brief at {path}"` and proceed to §3.
56	- Otherwise: emit the error-variant `SKF_BRIEF_RESULT_JSON` envelope (see §4b) on stderr with `halt_reason: "overwrite-cancelled"`, exit code 5, and HALT.
57	
58	If the file does not exist, proceed normally.
59	
60	### 3. Generate skill-brief.yaml
61	
62	**Resolve the `version` field per `{versionResolutionFile}` precedence:**
63	
64	- `target_version` (set in step 01) wins outright when present.
65	- Otherwise: auto-detected version from step 02.
66	- Otherwise: `"1.0.0"`.
67	
68	The reference's invariant section is binding: when `target_version` is set, the written brief MUST satisfy `brief.target_version == brief.version`. Set both fields to the identical string. Different values are a contract violation.
69	
70	Generate the YAML file using the approved field values and the schema template:
71	
72	```yaml
73	---
74	name: "{approved skill name}"
75	version: "{resolved version — target_version if set, else detected source version, else 1.0.0}"
76	source_type: "{source or docs-only}"
77	source_repo: "{approved source repo or doc site URL}"
78	language: "{approved language}"
79	description: "{approved description}"
80	forge_tier: "{approved forge tier}"
81	created: "{current date}"
82	created_by: "{user_name}"
83	scope:
84	  type: "{approved scope type}"
85	  include:
86	    - "{approved include patterns}"
87	  exclude:
88	    - "{approved exclude patterns}"
89	  notes: "{approved scope notes or empty string}"
90	---
91	```
92	
93	**Conditional optional field inclusion:**
94	
95	**If `target_version` was set in step 01:**
96	Include the `target_version` field in the generated YAML — its value MUST be identical to the `version` field above:
97	```yaml
98	target_version: "{target_version — same value as version}"
99	```
100	
101	**If `source_type: "docs-only"` OR supplemental `doc_urls` were collected:**
102	Include the `doc_urls` array (uncommented) in the generated YAML:
103	```yaml
104	doc_urls:
105	  - url: "{documentation URL}"
106	    label: "{page label}"
107	```
108	When `source_type: "docs-only"`: `doc_urls` is required (at least one entry), `source_repo` may be set to the doc site URL for reference or omitted.
109	
110	**If `scripts_intent` was collected and is not the default `"detect"`:**
111	Include the `scripts_intent` field (uncommented):
112	```yaml
113	scripts_intent: "{none or description}"
114	```
115	
116	**If `assets_intent` was collected and is not the default `"detect"`:**
117	Include the `assets_intent` field (uncommented):
118	```yaml
119	assets_intent: "{none or description}"
120	```
121	
122	**Always include** `source_authority` (default: `"community"`, forced to `"community"` when `source_type: "docs-only"`):
123	```yaml
124	source_authority: "{official|community|internal}"
125	```
126	
127	### 4. Write the File
128	
129	Write the generated YAML to `{forge_data_folder}/{skill-name}/skill-brief.yaml`.
130	
131	If write fails:
132	- Interactive: **HALT** — "**Error:** Failed to write skill-brief.yaml. Please check that the directory is writable and try again."
133	- Headless: emit the error-variant `SKF_BRIEF_RESULT_JSON` envelope (see §4b) on stderr with `halt_reason: "write-failed"`, exit code 4, and HALT.
134	
135	### 4b. Headless Result Envelope
136	
137	If `{headless_mode}` is true, emit a single-line JSON envelope on **stdout** immediately after the successful write (before §5 / §6 / §7) so pipeline schedulers can parse the outcome without grepping the prose summary:
138	
139	```
140	SKF_BRIEF_RESULT_JSON: {"status":"success","brief_path":"{abs_path}","skill_name":"{name}","version":"{resolved version}","language":"{language}","scope_type":"{scope.type}","exit_code":0,"halt_reason":null}
141	```
142	
143	The envelope shape on HARD HALT (any phase, written to **stderr**) uses the same keys with `status: "error"`, `brief_path: null` when no file was written, the matching `exit_code` from the table in `SKILL.md`, and `halt_reason` set to one of: `"input-missing"`, `"forge-tier-missing"`, `"target-inaccessible"`, `"gh-auth-failed"`, `"write-failed"`, `"overwrite-cancelled"`.
144	
145	When `{headless_mode}` is false, skip this section silently — no envelope is emitted.
146	
147	### 5. QMD Collection Registration (Deep Tier Only)
148	
149	**IF forge tier is Deep AND QMD tool is available:**
150	
151	Index the skill brief into a QMD collection so that portfolio-level searches can find existing briefs and avoid duplicate skill creation across large monorepos.
152	
153	**Collection creation:**
154	
155	Create a QMD collection targeting only the brief file:
156	```bash
157	qmd collection add {forge_data_folder}/{skill-name} --name {skill-name}-brief --mask "skill-brief.yaml"
158	qmd embed
159	```
160	
161	If collection already exists (re-briefing): remove and recreate for atomic replace:
162	```bash
163	qmd collection remove {skill-name}-brief
164	qmd collection add {forge_data_folder}/{skill-name} --name {skill-name}-brief --mask "skill-brief.yaml"
165	qmd embed
166	```
167	
168	**Embed verification:**
169	
170	After `qmd embed` completes, verify the collection was embedded:
171	- Run `qmd status` or `qmd collection list` and confirm `{skill-name}-brief` shows document count > 0
172	- If verification succeeds: proceed to registry update normally
173	- If verification fails: log warning "QMD embed verification failed for {skill-name}-brief — collection may not be searchable yet", still proceed to registry update but add `status: "pending"` field to the registry entry
174	
175	**Registry update:**
176	
177	Read `{sidecar_path}/forge-tier.yaml` and update the `qmd_collections` array.
178	
179	If an entry with `name: "{skill-name}-brief"` already exists, replace it. Otherwise, append:
180	
181	```yaml
182	  - name: "{skill-name}-brief"
183	    type: "brief"
184	    source_workflow: "brief-skill"
185	    skill_name: "{skill-name}"
186	    created_at: "{current ISO date}"
187	    # status: "pending"    # Added only when embed verification fails
188	```
189	
190	Write the updated forge-tier.yaml.
191	
192	**Error handling:**
193	- If QMD collection creation fails: log the error. Do NOT fail the workflow — the brief file was already written successfully.
194	- If forge-tier.yaml update fails: log the error, continue.
195	
196	**IF forge tier is NOT Deep:** Skip this section silently. No messaging.
197	
198	### 6. Display Success Summary
199	
200	"**Skill brief written successfully.**
201	
202	---
203	
204	**File:** `{forge_data_folder}/{skill-name}/skill-brief.yaml`
205	**Skill:** {name}
206	**Language:** {language}
207	**Scope:** {scope type}
208	**Forge Tier:** {forge tier}
209	
210	---
211	
212	## Next Steps
213	
214	Your skill brief is ready. To compile the actual skill from this brief, run:
215	
216	**create-skill** — Reads your skill-brief.yaml and compiles a complete SKILL.md with AST-backed analysis.
217	
218	After compilation, you can:
219	- **test-skill** — Validate the compiled skill
220	- **export-skill** — Package the skill for distribution
221	
222	---
223	
224	**Brief-skill workflow complete.**"
225	
226	### 7. Chain to Health Check
227	
228	ONLY WHEN the brief file has been written and the success summary displayed will you then load, read the full file, and execute `{nextStepFile}`. The health-check step is the true terminal step — do not stop here even though the summary reads as final.
229	
230	## CRITICAL STEP COMPLETION NOTE
231	
232	This step chains to the local health-check step (`{nextStepFile}`), which in turn delegates to `shared/health-check.md`. After the health check completes, the brief-skill workflow is fully done.
233	
234	
```
